# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-18)

## Corpus Check
- 4201 files · ~10,377,266 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 84 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 28, .css 15, .entitlements 8)

## Summary
- 56479 nodes · 166976 edges · 1443 communities (975 shown, 468 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4667 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `a6e064e9`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
